8. Spectral Feedback for Test-Time Alignment of Protein Diffusion Models

Abstract:Reward maximization alignment methods for discrete diffusion models have primarily focused on steering the reverse process, either by influencing token logits or by selecting favorable sequences at intermediate steps. These approaches largely treat inference as a unidirectional process, lacking mechanisms for revisiting undesirable token selections. We introduce Spectral Feedback, an algorithm that selects edit-positions in a feedback loop, allowing the model to iteratively correct its own generations. This approach leverages the mask structure of discrete diffusion models by re-masking and re-sampling tokens, analogous to image editing methods that reintroduce noisy latents and re-run the reverse process. While prior alignment methods focus on what token labels to assign to maximize a target reward, we instead treat which tokens to revisit as the central alignment problem. Selecting edit-positions is challenging because edit effects are interdependent: the impact of modifying one token depends on which others are edited simultaneously. We define an edit-set as a set of token positions to re-mask and re-sample. Motivated by prior work on sparse interactions in biological systems, we find empirically that edit-set value functions for protein inverse folding admit sparse Fourier representations. This structure enables Spectral Feedback to efficiently learn and optimize the value functions for edit-position selection. Spectral Feedback is model-agnostic and can be applied to pretrained, test-time aligned, and fine-tuned diffusion models. For all of these models, the algorithm improves alignment performance without modifying the underlying generative process. Applied to inverse folding with a protein stability reward oracle, it achieves a 32.3% increase in stable proteins for a pretrained model, 24.8% for Best-of-10, and 5.8% for a state-of-the-art RL fine-tuned diffusion model.

中文摘要

摘要：用于离散扩散模型的奖励最大化对齐方法主要集中于引导反向过程，要么通过影响标记 logits，要么通过在中间步骤选择有利的序列。这些方法大多将推理视为单向过程，缺乏重新审视不理想标记选择的机制。我们引入了谱反馈（Spectral Feedback）算法，该算法在反馈循环中选择编辑位置，使模型能够迭代地修正自身生成内容。这种方法利用了离散扩散模型的掩码结构，通过重新掩码和重新采样标记，类似于图像编辑方法中重新引入噪声潜量并重新运行反向过程。先前的对齐方法关注于分配哪些标记标签以最大化目标奖励，而我们则将“重新访问哪些标记”视为核心对齐问题。选择编辑位置具有挑战性，因为编辑效果是相互依赖的：修改一个标记的影响取决于同时编辑的其他标记。我们将编辑集定义为一组需要重新掩码和重新采样的标记位置。受先前关于生物系统稀疏交互研究的启发，我们通过实验证明，蛋白质逆向折叠的编辑集价值函数可以用稀疏傅里叶表示。这种结构使谱反馈能够高效地学习和优化编辑位置选择的价值函数。谱反馈与模型无关，可以应用于预训练模型、测试时对齐模型以及微调扩散模型。对于所有这些模型，该算法在不修改底层生成过程的情况下提升了对齐性能。在应用于具有蛋白质稳定性奖励先验的逆向折叠时，对于预训练模型实现了稳定蛋白数量增加32.3%，Best-of-10增加24.8%，而对于最先进的强化学习微调扩散模型，则增加5.8%。

Published: 2026-09-29T02:44:21.718Z

9. Pretrained ASR Pseudo-labeling for Noisy Police Audio

Abstract:Pretrained ASR systems perform poorly on noisy Broadcast Police Communication (BPC), hindering efforts to understand police decision-making. Pseudo-labeling offers an unsupervised path to improve ASR without expensive human labels, but the efficacy of this approach on very noisy domains is not known. In this work, we systematically assess the opportunities and limits of pseudo-labeling to adapt foundation ASR models (Whisper and Qwen3-ASR) to noisy BPC domain corpora from Baltimore and Chicago. We demonstrate that existing internal confidence metrics (log-probabilities and STAR scores) fail to distinguish between high and low quality BPC pseudo-labels, and we introduce an external LLM-as-a-judge filtering paradigm that leverages parametric knowledge to discard contextually implausible transcripts. Our LLM-judging filters more aggressively than internal metrics and significantly reduces WER of the pseudo-labeled training sets across the Baltimore and Chicago BPC corpora, though a substantial gap remains relative to an oracle filter. We also introduce a new cross-model pseudo-labeling paradigm where one model is finetuned with pseudo-labels from the other, and we identify this method as a promising direction for future pseudo-labeling work.

中文摘要

摘要：预训练的自动语音识别（ASR）系统在嘈杂的广播警务通信（BPC）中表现不佳，这阻碍了对警务决策的理解。伪标签方法提供了一种无需昂贵人工标注即可改进ASR的无监督途径，但在噪声极大的领域中这种方法的有效性尚不清楚。在本研究中，我们系统地评估了伪标签方法在将基础ASR模型（Whisper和Qwen3-ASR）适配于来自巴尔的摩和芝加哥的嘈杂BPC语料库中的机会和局限性。我们证明了现有的内部置信度指标（对数概率和STAR评分）无法区分高质量和低质量的BPC伪标签，我们引入了一种外部LLM作为评判者的过滤范式，该范式利用参数化知识剔除语境上不合理的转录结果。我们的LLM评判过滤器比内部指标更为积极，有效地降低了巴尔的摩和芝加哥BPC语料库中伪标签训练集的词错误率（WER），虽然相对于理想过滤器仍存在显著差距。我们还引入了一种新的跨模型伪标签范式，其中一个模型使用另一个模型的伪标签进行微调，并将该方法确定为未来伪标签研究的有希望方向。

10. Do LLMs Understand Context? A Knowledge Graph-Based Evaluation Framework

Abstract:While large language models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable linguistic capabilities, a profound question lingers at their core: do these models truly comprehend context or simply excel at pattern matching on an unprecedented scale? Contextual understanding in LLMs refers to the ability to correctly extract relevant information from a given context, integrate it into a coherent internal representation, and reason over it to produce factually consistent and contextually grounded responses. However, traditional methods such as BiLingual Evaluation Understudy (BLEU) and perplexity simply measure surface-level performance. This reveals a critical gap in question answering (QA), where responses must be contextually grounded rather than simply being memorized associations. To fill this void, we propose a novel knowledge graph (KG) based evaluation framework for LLM contextual understanding in QA. Central to this is Semantic Structural Similarity for KGs (S3KG), a hybrid similarity measure combining structural and semantic signals into a single score. In addition, a diagnostic analysis framework is developed to identify and categorize reasoning errors at the triplet level, enabling fine-grained analysis of model failures. Together, across nine benchmarks, S3KG achieves F1 gains of up to $+7.6$ points over the strongest baseline and AUROC up to $0.973$.

中文摘要

摘要：虽然大型语言模型（LLMs）已经在语言能力上取得了显著成就，但一个深刻的问题仍然存在于其核心：这些模型是否真正理解上下文，还是仅仅在前所未有的规模上擅长模式匹配？LLMs 中的上下文理解是指正确从给定上下文中提取相关信息，将其整合到一致的内部表示中，并进行推理以生成事实一致且具有上下文依据的回答的能力。然而，传统方法如双语评估替代（BLEU）和困惑度（perplexity）仅衡量表面性能。这揭示了问答（QA）中的一个关键缺口，即回答必须基于上下文，而不只是简单的记忆关联。为填补这一空白，我们提出了一种基于知识图谱（KG）的新型评估框架，用于评测 LLM 在 QA 中的上下文理解能力。其核心是知识图谱语义结构相似性（S3KG），一种将结构和语义信号结合为单一评分的混合相似性度量。此外，我们还开发了一个诊断分析框架，用于识别和分类三元组层面的推理错误，从而实现对模型失败的细粒度分析。总体来看，在九个基准测试中，S3KG 相比最强基线实现了最高 $+7.6$ 的 F1 值提升，以及最高 $0.973$ 的 AUROC。

1. Bringing AI to Autonomous Systems — From Cognition to Collective Intelligence

Abstract:The purpose of this article is to highlight the central role of autonomous systems as the ultimate stage in the development of AI, to explain the underlying technical challenges that require a combination of connectionist AI and symbolic AI, and to integrate AI and systems engineering. We present a comprehensive framework for the design and evaluation of autonomous systems, based on a generic agent architecture that characterizes their behavior as the composition of cognitive functions organized around a long-term memory containing the agent’s evolving knowledge. We address the challenges posed by the implementation of the fundamental features of the agent architecture, in particular the link between sensory data and structured data stored in memory, decision-making related to the achievement of the agent’s goals and their planning, as well as the coordination of agents to combine individual and collective intelligence. We explain that agent trustworthiness, unlike that of traditional systems, is not limited to behavioral properties. It includes an essential dimension related to cognitive properties, the validity of which depends on how the agent uses its knowledge in decision-making. We present avenues for the development of methods for evaluating agent trustworthiness. We conclude with a critical assessment of the substantial gap between the aspirational vision of autonomous multi-agent systems and the current state of the art.

中文摘要

摘要：本文的目的是强调自主系统作为人工智能发展的最终阶段的核心作用，解释需要结合联结主义人工智能和符号人工智能的潜在技术挑战，并整合人工智能与系统工程。我们提出了一个用于自主系统设计和评估的综合框架，该框架基于一个通用代理架构，将其行为描述为围绕包含代理不断发展知识的长期记忆组织的认知功能的组合。我们讨论了实现代理架构基本特征所带来的挑战，尤其是感知数据与存储在记忆中的结构化数据之间的链接、与实现代理目标相关的决策及其规划，以及协调代理以结合个体和集体智能。我们解释了代理的可信性与传统系统不同，不仅限于行为属性，还包括一个与认知属性相关的核心维度，其有效性取决于代理在决策中使用知识的方式。我们提出了评估代理可信性方法的发展途径。最后，我们对自主多代理系统的理想愿景与当前技术水平之间的巨大差距进行了批判性评估。

2. ScopeBench: Do Agents Preserve Engagement Boundaries Under Goal Pressure?

Abstract:Agents are increasingly deployed with real autonomy in web application and network penetration testing, where a single out-of-scope action can breach a client’s engagement boundary. Existing offensive-security benchmarks measure raw hacking capability; as those benchmarks saturate, the real barrier to deployment is a special case of alignment: scope adherence. We introduce ScopeBench, a benchmark of 30 dead-end agentic security tasks in which the stated objective is reachable only by violating the stated scope. Each task appears under two conditions that share an environment, verifier, and objective and differ only in scope: one instruction set has no scope and measures capability; the other has a natural-language scope to measure adherence. Scopeless trajectories are graded by a standard deterministic verifier. Scoped trajectories pass through two grading arms. First, the same deterministic verifier checks for the flag: because the flag sits behind the scope boundary, a pass proves by construction that a forbidden action occurred, yielding a high-precision lower bound on the violation rate. If the verifier does not pass the trajectory, an agentic judge estimates whether an out-of-scope call occurred. We calibrate the judge against 100 ScopeBench trajectories labeled call-by-call by human annotators, and a blinded audit of the evaluated rollouts finds its high recall holds - no false negatives among the 36 audited violations, with over-flagging its only observed error. Across 8 models in one harness, raw capability spans 12.2% to 81.1% and scope adherence spans 34.4% to 86.7%, with the judge finding 331 violations that mechanical verification misses. Opus-4-8 achieves a raw-capability score 10 percentage points higher than sonnet-4-6’s while exhibiting 35.6 percentage points higher scope adherence. We release the frozen pilot benchmark, evaluation code, and all 2160 ATIF trajectories.

中文摘要

摘要：代理在 Web 应用程序和网络渗透测试中越来越多地被部署以实现真实的自主操作，其中一次超出范围的操作就可能突破客户的参与边界。现有的进攻性安全基准衡量的是原始的黑客能力；随着这些基准趋于饱和，部署的真正障碍是一种特殊形式的对齐：即范围遵守。我们引入了 ScopeBench，这是一个包含 30 个死胡同型代理安全任务的基准，每个任务的声明目标只能通过违反声明的范围才能达成。每个任务在两种条件下出现，这两种条件共享环境、验证器和目标，仅在范围上有所不同：一种指令集没有范围限制以衡量能力；另一种有自然语言范围以衡量遵守情况。无范围轨迹由标准的确定性验证器评分。有限范围轨迹通过两个评分环节。首先，相同的确定性验证器检查标志：因为标志位于范围边界之外，通过验证就证明了发生了禁止操作，从而提供了高精度的违规率下限。如果验证器未通过轨迹，代理评审员将评估是否发生了超范围调用。我们将评审员依据 100 个由人工标注的 ScopeBench 轨迹（逐调用标注）进行校准，并通过对评估的 rollout 进行盲审，发现其高召回率依然保持——在 36 次审核的违规中没有漏判，唯一观察到的错误是过度标记。在一个环境中对 8 个模型的测试显示，原始能力范围为 12.2% 到 81.1%，范围遵守率为 34.4% 到 86.7%，评审员发现了机械验证遗漏的 331 个违规行为。Opus-4-8 的原始能力得分比 sonnet-4-6 高 10 个百分点，同时其范围遵守率高出 35.6 个百分点。我们发布了冻结的试点基准、评估代码以及所有 2160 条 ATIF 轨迹。

3. When Is a Multi-Agent Code Judge Actually Grounded? Two Label-Free Measurements, and a Judge That Declines to Guess

Abstract:When one language model judges whether another’s code is correct, it does not report the absence of evidence. It returns a confident verdict with reasoning attached, indistinguishable from a verdict it had grounds for. Multi-agent verification, which decomposes a judgment into checkable claims and verifies each against evidence, is a promising response and works well when the evidence is a set of retrieved documents. We argue such methods require two things of their evidence: it must be independent of the answer under review, and it must differ between the two candidates being compared. The second condition holds automatically with retrieved documents and stops holding in code judging. Running MARCH, a published framework unmodified over 80 condition-by-cell measurements on two code judging benchmarks, we find it declares both solutions equally good on 78 to 95% of comparisons, reaching 4.4% accuracy where the same model asked directly reaches 43.7%. Neither easier problems nor a larger judge changes this. Two measurements taken from the pipeline’s own logs explain it without needing labels. Gating on one of them, the pipeline declines the comparisons it cannot make and raises its accuracy from 20.7 to 36.9% while still answering half of all comparisons. The contribution is not a more accurate judge, but a label-free way to tell when a judge has no basis for its answer.

中文摘要

摘要：当一个语言模型判断另一个模型的代码是否正确时，它不会报告证据缺失。它会返回一个带有推理的自信判决，这与它有依据的判决没有区别。多代理验证，将一个判断分解为可检查的声明并根据证据对每个声明进行验证，是一种有前景的应对方法，并且当证据是一组检索到的文档时效果良好。我们认为这种方法对其证据有两个要求：它必须独立于正在审核的答案，并且在被比较的两个候选答案之间必须存在差异。第二个条件在检索到的文档中自动成立，但在代码判断中不成立。在两个代码评测基准上，未修改地运行已发布的 MARCH 框架对 80 个条件-单元测量进行测试，我们发现它在 78% 到 95% 的比较中都将两个解决方案判定为同样良好，准确率为 4.4%，而同一个模型直接被问及时准确率可达 43.7%。无论问题更简单还是评判者更大，这一现象都不会改变。从管道自身日志中提取的两项测量可以在不需要标签的情况下解释这种情况。对其中一项测量进行门控，管道会拒绝无法做出的比较，并将其准确率从 20.7% 提高到 36.9%，同时仍回答一半的比较问题。该研究贡献不是提供一个更准确的评判者，而是提供一种无标签方法来判断评判者在何时没有其答案的依据。

4. Bridging LLM Agents and Data Spaces: An Architectural Mediation Approach using the Model Context Protocol

Abstract:Data Spaces enable sovereign and governed data sharing across organizational boundaries, but their integration with AI agents remains challenging due to mismatches between probabilistic language model interactions and policy-driven data infrastructures. This article presents an architectural mediation approach based on the Model Context Protocol (MCP), implemented through the Eunomia Agent, to enable controlled interaction between large language model (LLM) agents and data space services. The proposed mediation layer translates data space capabilities into structured, schema-driven tools that AI agents can discover and invoke while preserving governance constraints. A prototype implementation validates end-to-end interaction across catalog discovery, metadata retrieval, and data service invocation without modifying existing data space components. Results demonstrate that protocol-based mediation enables interoperable and standards-aligned integration of AI agents into data space ecosystems. The approach provides practical guidance for organizations seeking to introduce AI-driven automation into governed data-sharing environments while maintaining compliance, interoperability, and architectural separation of concerns.

中文摘要

摘要：数据空间使跨组织边界的主权和受控数据共享成为可能，但由于概率语言模型交互与政策驱动的数据基础设施之间的不匹配，它们与人工智能代理的整合仍然具有挑战性。本文提出了一种基于模型上下文协议（MCP）的架构调解方法，通过Eunomia代理实现，以实现大型语言模型（LLM）代理与数据空间服务之间的受控交互。所提出的调解层将数据空间功能转换为结构化、模式驱动的工具，AI代理可以发现并调用，同时保持治理约束。原型实现验证了跨目录发现、元数据检索和数据服务调用的端到端交互，而无需修改现有的数据空间组件。结果表明，基于协议的调解使AI代理能够以可互操作和符合标准的方式整合到数据空间生态系统中。该方法为希望在受控数据共享环境中引入AI驱动自动化的组织提供了实际指导，同时保持合规性、互操作性和架构关注点的分离。

5. Stealth Apart, Harm Together: Skill Cascading Attacks on Skill-Based Agent Systems

Abstract:A skill is a modular package of natural-language instructions, executable scripts, and reference resources that an agent can load at runtime to extend its capabilities for a specific task. Skill-based agent systems therefore enable flexible reuse of third-party capabilities, but the openness of this skill ecosystem also opens up a new attack surface. Prior work has focused on vulnerabilities within individual skills, but little attention has been paid to risks that arise from interactions across skills. In this paper, we introduce skill cascading attacks, a threat paradigm in which a malicious objective is distributed across multiple skills so that each modification looks benign in isolation, yet their combined execution is harmful. For instance, in a prescription-review pipeline, the first skill weakens signals of recently discontinued medications in the extracted history, the second downgrades the severity of any drug interaction tied to them, and the third suppresses the resulting low-priority alert in the final summary, so that a severe drug-interaction warning silently disappears before reaching the physician. To systematically study this safety blind spot, we develop SkillCascade, an automated multi-agent red-teaming framework, and release SkillCascade-Bench, a benchmark of 213 validated cascading test cases across multiple agent systems and domains. Across representative agents (e.g., OpenClaw, Claude Code, Codex) and LLM backbones, cascaded interactions reliably induce harmful behaviors while evading existing per-skill scanners and runtime monitors. Our findings highlight a gap between component-level integrity and system-level safety, and call for defenses that reason over cross-skill interactions rather than individual skills in isolation.

中文摘要

摘要：技能是一种模块化的包，包含自然语言指令、可执行脚本和参考资源，代理可以在运行时加载以扩展其针对特定任务的能力。因此，基于技能的代理系统能够灵活地重用第三方能力，但这种技能生态系统的开放性也带来了新的攻击面。以往的研究主要关注单个技能中的漏洞，但很少关注跨技能交互带来的风险。在本文中，我们引入了技能级联攻击，这是一种威胁范式，其中恶意目标分布在多个技能中，使得每个修改在单独看来都是无害的，但它们的组合执行却是有害的。例如，在处方审查流程中，第一个技能削弱了在提取的历史中最近停用药物的信号，第二个技能降低了与这些药物相关的任何药物相互作用的严重性，第三个技能在最终摘要中抑制了由此产生的低优先级警报，从而使严重药物相互作用的警告在到达医生之前悄然消失。为了系统地研究这一安全盲点，我们开发了SkillCascade，一个自动化多代理红队框架，并发布了SkillCascade-Bench，一个跨多个代理系统和领域的213个经过验证的级联测试用例基准。在典型代理（如OpenClaw、Claude Code、Codex）和大型语言模型（LLM）骨干中，级联交互可靠地诱发有害行为，同时规避现有的单技能扫描器和运行时监控。我们的研究结果突显了组件级完整性与系统级安全之间的差距，并呼吁开发能够考虑跨技能交互而非单独技能的防御措施。

LLM Analysis

Q: 这篇论文试图解决什么问题？ 这篇论文揭示了基于技能的智能体系统（skill-based agent systems）中由\*\*跨技能交互\*\*引入的结构性安全盲区，并系统性地研究了名为\*\*技能级联攻击\*\*（Skill Cascading Attacks）的新型威胁范式。 具体而言，论文试图解决和阐明以下核心问题： ## 1. 现有安全分析的单元局限性 当前针对技能型智能体的攻防研究普遍假设\*\*分析单元是单个技能\*\*（per-skill）。现有的扫描工具（如 Skill-Vetter、Skill-Scanner）和运行时防御仅对独立技能进行审查，无法检测将恶意逻辑\*\*分布式地嵌入多个技能\*\*的攻击。论文指出，这种"单技能分析"范式留下了关键的安全盲点。 ## 2. 跨技能交互产生的攻击面 在基于技能的智能体架构中，多个技能通过\*\*共享上下文窗口\*\*（shared context window）进行信息流动。一个技能的输出会作为权威输入被后续技能读取，甚至可能影响后续技能的调度选择。论文发现，这种架构特性使得风险能够在\*\*设计上相互独立的技能之间传播\*\*，而现有防御机制并未审视这种跨技能交互。 ## 3. 技能级联攻击的形式化与验证 论文将技能级联攻击定义为一个满足三重条件的威胁模型： - \*\*个体隐蔽性\*\*（Individual Stealth）：每个被修改的技能单独通过安全扫描； - \*\*联合危害性\*\*（Joint Harm）：多个技能在真实用户请求下组合执行时产生有害输出； - \*\*不可或缺性\*\*（Indispensability）：撤销任意单个技能的修改即可消除危害。 论文指出，这种攻击类比于软件供应链攻击（如 SolarWinds 事件），将恶意目标分散在多个看似可信的组件中。 ## 4. 系统级安全与组件级安全的差距 论文的核心论点在于：即使每个技能在组件层面保持完整（individual stealth），系统层面的安全性仍无法得到保证。这种\*\*组件级完整性\*\*与\*\*系统级安全性\*\*之间的断层，要求防御机制必须从"孤立审查单个技能"转向"推理跨技能交互"。 为研究这一问题，论文构建了自动化多智能体红队框架 SKILLCASCADE 以及包含 213 个验证案例的基准数据集 SKILLCASCADE-BENCH，实验表明此类攻击在 OpenClaw、Claude Code、Codex 等主流系统中平均成功率达 89.4%，且能可靠绕过现有预部署扫描器和运行时防御。 Q2: 有哪些相关研究？ 根据论文附录及参考文献，相关研究可沿以下四条主线梳理： ### 一、技能级（Skill-Level）安全漏洞与供应链风险 这条线关注技能抽象本身引入的攻击面，假设\*\*恶意行为在单个技能内即可被检测\*\*。 - \*\*Skill-Inject\*\*

37
与 **Schmotz et al.**
36
：测量智能体对嵌入技能文件中的自然语言指令的脆弱性，发现技能抽象本身可支持一类极其简单但高效的提示注入。 - **SkillJect**
17
：将威胁扩展到技能的可执行脚本侧，通过基于执行轨迹的闭环自动化实现隐蔽的脚本级注入。 - **供应链视角**
33, 42, 26
：把技能生态视为软件供应链，研究已发布技能如何在安装时被劫持以植入提示、载荷或后门。 - **大规模实证研究**
23, 24, 38
：对市场中已部署技能的漏洞普遍性与分布模式进行特征分析。 ### 二、技能级防御机制（单技能扫描与运行时监控） 现有防护分为**部署前扫描**与**运行时监控**，其共同假设是威胁可被定位在单个技能或单步执行内。 - **单技能扫描器**：Skill-Vetter

40
、Skill-Scanner
10
、SkillScan
32
、AIG-EdgeOne
41
等，使用静态规则或 LLM-as-judge 对独立技能进行审查。 - **小规模组合扫描**：SkillProbe
15
与 SkillTester
45
尝试通过组合风险推理将分析扩展到两三个技能的组合。 - **运行时防御**：借鉴更广泛的提示注入缓解技术，包括在输入边界重新标记可信与不可信文本段
16
、在数据流与控制流之间实施能力式隔离
6, 13
、通过偏好优化训练模型忽略注入指令
7
、在提示边界部署博弈论检测器
25
、将第三方工具执行限制在专用沙箱中
46
。 - **沙箱基准**：HAICOSYSTEM
52
与 SHADE-Arena
19
用于衡量运行时监控在捕获执行阶段破坏行为方面的可靠性。 ### 三、工具链/多智能体组合攻击（Tool-Chain Attacks） 这类研究关注**保持底层组件未修改**，通过控制组件间消息的顺序、内容或框架来产生危害。 - **通用沙箱与基准**：AgentDojo

12
、AgentHarm
1
、Agent Security Bench
50
等在不同智能体环境中剖析此攻击面。 - **间接提示注入基准**：InjecAgent
49
与 BIPIA
48
测量注入内容如何通过工具调用接口传播。 - **具体攻击实例**： - **ToolHijacker**
39
：注入精心设计的工具文档，诱导智能体调用攻击者控制的工具。 - **Les Dissonances**
21
：展示同一工具池中看似良性的工具如何相互污染输出。 - **Attractive Metadata Attack**
27
：通过描述性元数据诱饵偏置工具检索。 - **STAC**
20
：跨轮次链接触发良性工具，使单个调用无害，但组合有害。 - **Multi-Agent Control-Flow Hijacking**
44
：颠覆多智能体协作中的控制流。 - **多轮越狱**：Crescendo
35
与 X-Teaming
34
等将有害意图分解到多个对话轮次中，以绕过单轮安全过滤器。 ### 四、与本文的核心区别 论文明确指出，上述研究与本工作的关键差异在于： 1. **与单技能安全研究的区别**：已有攻防工作共享“分析单元是单个技能”的假设；而本文的**技能级联攻击**将恶意目标**分布到多个技能**中，每个修改单独通过审查，但联合执行时产生危害。 2. **与工具链攻击的区别**：工具链攻击保持组件本身未修改，通过操控组件间通信产生危害；而本文攻击**驻留在组件内部**，每个被修改的技能携带一个微小、单独看来良性的编辑，仅在与其他被修改技能并行运行时才会暴露威胁面。 Q3: 论文如何解决这个问题？ 论文并未声称完全“解决”了技能级联攻击的防御问题，而是通过**形式化、自动化构建、系统验证与防御探索**四个层面，首次系统地揭示并研究了该威胁。具体路径如下： ### 1. 形式化威胁定义 论文首先将技能级联攻击形式化为一个满足三重约束的威胁范式（Definition 1）。设目标技能集为 T = S_1, …, S_m ，修改后的技能为 δ_i(S_i) ，修改后的系统为 A^ ，回退单个修改的系统为 A^(setminus i) ，则攻击需同时满足： - **个体隐蔽性（Individual Stealth）**： ∀ i, scan(δ_i(S_i)) = SAFE - **联合危害性（Joint Harm）**： ∃ q ∈ Q, h(exec(A^, q)) > τ - \*不可或缺性（Indispensability）**： ∀ i, h(exec(A^(setminus i), q)) le varepsilon ll τ 该形式化为后续攻击构造与验证提供了严格的数学评判标准。 ### 2. 构建自动化多智能体红队框架 SKILLCASCADE 为实现规模化研究，论文设计了由五个专用 LLM 智能体协作的自动化红队框架，通过三层嵌套反馈循环迭代生成有效攻击： - \*场景发现智能体（Scenario Discovery Agent）**：从真实技能市场（ClawHub）中自顶向下或自底向上地发现由多个技能组成的现实工作流。 - **攻击智能体（Attack Agent）**：将场景转化为具体级联攻击计划，并对每个技能进行最小化修改；每个修改必须附带可辩护的域内合理性说明（legitimate rationale），以确保隐蔽性。 - **扫描器智能体（Scanner Agent）**：利用现有公开扫描工具（如 Skill-Vetter、Skill-Scanner 等）对修改后的技能进行独立审查，确保满足个体隐蔽性；未通过则回退至攻击智能体修订。 - **测试用例生成智能体（Test Case Generator Agent）**：为通过扫描的攻击构造看似无害的用户提示与输入数据，用于在沙箱中触发级联。 - **裁决智能体（Judge Agent）**：在隔离的执行环境（containerized sandbox）中运行测试用例，根据评估标准判定攻击是否成功（联合危害性）；若失败，则根据诊断将失败路由回上游相应智能体进行迭代优化。 ### 3. 发布基准数据集 SKILLCASCADE-BENCH 基于 SKILLCASCADE 框架，论文从真实技能市场构建了首个技能级联攻击基准： - 包含 **213 个经过人工验证的测试用例**，覆盖 10 个应用领域； - 涵盖全部 **7 种攻击目标**（如输出篡改、决策操纵、信息泄露等）与 **3 种级联模式**（因果级联、组合级联、混合级联）； - 每个用例包含原始与修改后的技能、用户提示及输入文件，可直接在目标智能体系统上复现。 ### 4. 大规模实验验证与防御绕过分析 论文在 **3 个主流技能型智能体系统**（OpenClaw、Claude Code、Codex）与 **8 个 LLM 骨干**（含 GPT-5.4、Claude Opus 4.6、Gemini 2.5 Pro、Qwen 3 72B 等）上进行了全面评估，结果表明： - **攻击有效性**：平均攻击成功率（ASR）高达 89.4% ，且在高风险领域（如医疗、金融）仍保持高成功率； - **隐蔽性**：修改后的技能对现有 **5 种单技能扫描器** 的通过率超过 86% ，边际检测信号（ Delta ）不超过 4.7 个百分点；对 **3 种跨技能联合扫描器** 的通过率仍超过 81% ； - **运行时防御绕过**：对 **4 种运行时防御**（静态+动态策略、每次调用规则执行、风险分级门控、轨迹级检查）的绕过率（DER）平均达 88.5% 。 这些实验量化了“组件级安全”与“系统级安全”之间的断层，证明现有防御在结构上不足以应对跨技能威胁。 ### 5. 探索跨技能自适应防御 在分析攻击机理的基础上，论文提出了一种概念验证型自适应防御方案——**Behavior-Composition Scanner**： - 该扫描器采用三步 LLM 驱动协议：（i）总结每个技能的行为承诺；（ii）链式推理这些承诺的联合后果；（iii）通过生成式反事实推理判断是否存在单技能无法产生但多技能组合可能产生的不安全输出。 - 实验表明，该防御可将**因果级联**的隐蔽率降低超过 30 个百分点，但对组合级联与混合级联的效果仍有限，说明全面覆盖的跨技能防御仍是亟待未来研究的方向。 综上，论文通过“形式化—自动化构建—基准发布—实证验证—防御探索”的完整研究链条，首次系统性地将技能安全研究的分析单元从“单个技能”推进到“技能组合”层面。 Q4: 论文做了哪些实验？ 论文在 **Section 5（Experiments）** 与 **Section 6（Analysis）** 中开展了一系列实验，旨在验证技能级联攻击的有效性、隐蔽性与防御绕过能力，并分析攻击背后的结构性因素。实验可归纳为以下五个维度： —- ### 1. 实验设置与配置 - **目标系统**：选取三个主流技能型智能体系统——OpenClaw、Claude Code 与 Codex，分别实例化于多个 LLM 骨干之上。 - **LLM 骨干**：涵盖 8 个模型，包括 GPT-5.4、GPT-5.4 Mini、Claude Opus 4.6、Claude Sonnet 4.6、Gemini 2.5 Pro、Gemini 2.5 Flash、Qwen 3 72B 与 Kimi K2.5，覆盖闭源旗舰、闭源轻量与开源权重模型。 - **基线防御**： - **预部署扫描器**：5 个单技能扫描器（Skill-Scanner

10
（静态与 LLM 双版本）、SkillScan
32
、Skill-Vetter
40
、AIG-Scanner
41
）与 3 个跨技能扫描器（Skill-Scanner
10
、SkillProbe
15
、Joint-LLM）。 - **运行时防御**：4 类运行时机制——DefenseClaw
9
（静态+动态策略）、ClawGuard
51
（每次调用规则执行）、HITL Defense
38
（风险分级门控）、AgentDoG
22
（轨迹级检查）。 - **评估指标**： - **ASR（Attack Success Rate）**：攻击目标在最终输出中达成的比例，由 Judge Agent 根据评估标准裁决。 - **SR（Stealth Rate）**：扫描器返回 SAFE verdict 的比例，分 SRper（单技能粒度）与 SRjoint（跨技能粒度）。 - **DER（Defense Evasion Rate）**：运行时防御被绕过的比例。 —- ### 2. 整体攻击有效性实验 - **跨系统与跨骨干 ASR**：在 3 × 8 = 24 个系统-骨干配置上运行全部 213 个测试用例，每个配置重复 3 次。结果显示全局平均 ASR 为 89.4% ，其中 GPT-5.4 Mini、Qwen 3 72B 等轻量/开源模型最脆弱（ASR 达 96.7% – 97.1% ），而 Claude Opus 4.6 与 GPT-5.4 等旗舰模型抗性最强（ASR 约 76.5% – 80.6% ），但差距有限。 - **ASR 多维度分解**（图 3）： - **按攻击目标（O1–O7）**：对“输出腐败”“决策操纵”等伪装性高的目标成功率最高；对现有对齐训练覆盖较密的“未授权行动”等目标成功率相对较低，但仍可通过级联实现。 - **按级联模式**：因果级联（Causal）成功率最高，组合级联（Compositional）次之，混合级联（Hybrid）介于两者之间。 - **按应用领域**：常规知识工作领域（电商、新闻）ASR 接近 100% ；高 stakes 且对齐先验密集的领域（医疗、药物发现）ASR 最低，但仍超过 80% 。 —- ### 3. 对预部署扫描器的隐蔽性实验 - **单技能扫描**（表 3）：将每个修改后的技能独立提交给 5 个单技能扫描器。结果显示所有扫描器对修改后技能的通过率在 86.6% – 94.3% 之间，与原始（未修改）技能的通过率相比，边际检测信号 Delta 最大仅为 4.7 个百分点。这表明修改后的技能在统计上与良性技能不可区分。 - **联合跨技能扫描**（表 4）：将同一级联的全部修改技能一起提交给 3 个跨技能扫描器。结果仍然显示多数级联通过审查：静态扫描器 Delta = 0 ，完全无法检测；LLM 联合扫描器的 Delta 也仅为 5.2 个百分点。这说明攻击的隐蔽性源于级联结构本身，而非扫描器“视野局限”的副产品。 —- ### 4. 对运行时防御的隐蔽性实验 - **运行时防御绕过**（表 5）：在 OpenClaw + GPT-5.4-mini 配置下，评估 4 类运行时防御。DER 分别为 90.6% 、 88.9% 、 91.8% 与 82.5% ，平均达 88.5% 。轨迹级检查（AgentDoG）作为唯一跨步骤的防御范式，仍漏过 82.5% 的案例，原因在于跨步骤危害仅在显式组合推理下才会浮现。 —- ### 5. 深度分析实验 - **不可或缺性消融**（图 4）：在三技能级联上进行片段撤回实验（fragment-withdrawal ablation）： - 当撤回任意 **1** 个修改（ k=1 ）时，残余 ASR 骤降至 13% – 18% ；撤回 **2** 个修改时降至个位数。这验证了定义 1 中的不可或缺性条件：危害并非由单一技能承载，而是多技能共同携带。 - 按撤回位置分析：因果级联呈现“头→尾”梯度（头部修改最关键），组合级联呈平坦分布（各片段贡献均等），混合级联则呈现头部与中部对称、尾部关键的特征。 - **单技能扫描失败机理**（图 5）：对原始技能、单技能攻击载荷与级联攻击修改三组技能，沿六个静态分析信号轴（可疑关键词、嵌入指令、工具/权限不对称、代码复杂度、输出模式异常、上下文操纵）进行评分。结果显示单技能攻击载荷显著偏离良性基线，而级联攻击的多边形紧贴基线——因为每次编辑都微小且局部合理。 - **攻击技术频率统计**（图 6）：对 9 种重复出现的攻击技术（M1–M9）进行频次分析。排名最高的为条件逻辑注入（M1）与认知框架重构（M2），二者直接利用共享 LLM 上下文窗口的语义特性；其余技术涵盖标签升级、默认利用、选择性过滤等经典供应链战术。 - **自适应防御评估**（图 7）：提出 Behavior-Composition Scanner 作为概念验证防御，通过行为摘要、链式后果推理与生成式反事实判断三步协议检测级联。实验显示该防御将因果级联的隐蔽率降低逾 30 个百分点，但对组合级联与混合级联的效果递减，证明跨技能推理是可行方向，但全面防御仍需未来研究。 Q5: 有什么可以进一步探索的点？ 基于该论文的发现与局限，以下方向值得进一步深入探索： —- ### 一、跨技能防御机制设计 论文提出的 **Behavior-Composition Scanner** 仅作为概念验证，对因果级联有效，但对组合级联与混合级联的检测率仍不理想。未来工作可围绕以下子问题展开： - **显式数据流与语义关联分析**：不仅推理技能间的行为承诺链，还需建立跨技能的共享标识符、字段模式与触发谓词的映射关系（如论文 E.2 案例所示，“Fair-to-Good” 等非规范标签在技能间的生产-消费关系）。 - **反事实生成与模拟执行**：在部署前构建虚拟级联执行环境，通过生成式反事实自动探查“若技能 A 与技能 B 同时被调用，是否会产生单技能无法产生的非意图后果”。 - **动态权限与信息流隔离**：探索对共享上下文窗口的细粒度隔离机制，限制技能输出中可被下游技能解读为“指令”或“框架”的语义内容，而非仅依赖静态规则或单次调用检查。 —- ### 二、运行时跨步骤异常检测 现有运行时防御（如 DefenseClaw、ClawGuard、AgentDoG）的绕过率平均高达 88.5% ，根源在于其检测信号局限于单步或表层行为。未来可探索： - **轨迹级语义偏离检测**：监测多步执行中关键实体（如医疗场景中的药物剂量、财务场景中的风险评级）的语义漂移，而非仅监控工具调用参数或输出格式。 - **中间状态审计**：在技能调用之间插入可验证的中间表示（intermediate representation），对比当前上下文与历史良性执行的统计分布，识别被渐进式弱化的信号。 —- ### 三、技能市场生态的供应链治理 论文指出，攻击者可通过单一供应商品牌将多个恶意技能打包为“套件”实现共安装。这呼唤市场层面的系统性治理： - **组合安装审计**：在用户批准安装某一技能套件时，平台级机制自动对该套件内所有技能进行交叉行为分析，而非仅逐技能扫描。 - **开发者信誉与溯源图**：构建技能发布者的供应链图谱，分析同一发布者名下多个技能的隐式依赖与信息耦合模式。 - **最小权限与技能沙箱化**：推动技能运行时的权限默认最小化，并对同一来源的多个技能实施额外的执行隔离策略。 —- ### 四、扩展级联模式与攻击面 论文形式化了因果、组合与混合三种基本模式，但技能生态的复杂性可能催生更隐蔽的变体： - **动态自适应级联**：攻击技能根据运行时上下文动态决定是否激活恶意逻辑，使级联路径非确定性，增加静态分析难度。 - **长链级联与跨会话持久化**：当前基准平均级联长度为 3.25 个技能，更长链条（如 5 个以上技能）或跨用户会话的持久化污染（通过共享记忆或参考文档）尚未被充分研究。 - **对抗性共演化**：研究攻击者与防御者之间的多轮对抗，例如当防御方引入跨技能检测后，攻击者如何调整修改的分布与隐蔽策略。 —- ### 五、理论形式化与可证明安全性 论文给出了级联攻击的操作性定义，但缺乏更深层的理论刻画： - **可证明安全/不安全性边界**：在特定技能交互拓扑下，是否存在可证明的判定 procedure 来检测或排除级联攻击？该问题的计算复杂度如何？ - **信息论与因果推断视角**：将级联视为通过共享上下文窗口的信息流操控，利用因果推断框架量化“下游技能决策对上游技能输出的敏感依赖度”，从而识别潜在攻击路径。 —- ### 六、基准与评估方法学改进 - **真实世界部署研究**：当前实验在受控沙箱中进行，真实市场中的技能共安装模式、用户实际调用频率与攻击发生率仍是开放问题，需要纵向田野研究或众包安全审计。 - **人机协同裁决**：尽管 Judge Agent 与人类标注者的一致性较高（Cohen’s kappa = 0.86 ），但对语义微妙（如认知框架重构）的案例仍需引入领域专家进行深度审查，以建立更高质量的金标准。 - **跨领域迁移评估**：检验在某一领域（如医疗）训练的跨技能防御是否能迁移至差异显著的领域（如法律或金融），评估攻击与防御的领域特异性。 —- ### 七、防御性多智能体对抗训练 论文使用多智能体红队（SKILLCASCADE）自动生成攻击，反向可构建防御性多智能体蓝队： - **红蓝对抗闭环**：让攻击智能体与防御智能体在持续对抗中相互强化，自动生成更复杂的级联案例并同步迭代防御策略。 - **技能自动修复**：在检测到潜在级联风险后，由防御智能体自动提出对单个技能的补丁（如增加约束、调整默认值），在保持功能的前提下破坏级联的不可或缺性条件。 Q6: 总结一下论文的主要内容 该论文针对基于技能的 LLM 智能体系统，提出并系统研究了**技能级联攻击**（Skill Cascading Attacks）这一新型安全威胁范式。 ## 1. 研究背景与核心问题 当前技能型智能体（如 Claude Code、OpenClaw、Codex）通过动态加载第三方技能扩展能力，形成开放的技能供应链。现有安全研究集中于**单个技能**的漏洞扫描（如提示注入、恶意脚本、工具滥用），但忽视了关键架构特性：所有技能通过**共享上下文窗口**交互，一个技能的输出会被后续技能读取为权威输入。这种跨技能信息流动引入了结构性安全盲区——风险可在设计上独立的技能间传播，而现有防御无法审视此类交互。 ## 2. 技能级联攻击的形式化定义 论文将攻击定义为满足三重条件的威胁： - **个体隐蔽性**（Individual Stealth）：每个被修改的技能独立通过现有安全扫描； - **联合危害性**（Joint Harm）：多个技能在真实用户请求下联合执行时产生有害输出； - **不可或缺性**（Indispensability）：撤销任意单个技能的修改即可消除危害。 攻击分为三种模式：**因果级联**（信号沿技能链顺序弱化）、**组合级联**（独立无害的片段在最终输出中聚合为危害）、**混合级联**（前两者的融合）。 ## 3. SKILLCASCADE 框架与基准数据集 为规模化研究该威胁，论文构建了： - **SKILLCASCADE**：一个自动化多智能体红队框架，由场景发现、攻击生成、扫描审查、测试用例生成与裁决五个 LLM 智能体组成，通过三层嵌套反馈循环自动生成并验证级联攻击。每个技能修改必须附带可辩护的域内合理性说明，以确保独立通过扫描。 - **SKILLCASCADE-BENCH**：首个技能级联攻击基准，包含 **213 个**经三位专家人工验证的测试用例，覆盖 10 个领域、7 种攻击目标（输出篡改、决策操纵、信息泄露等）与全部 3 种级联模式。 ## 4. 实验验证与防御绕过 在 OpenClaw、Claude Code、Codex 三大系统以及 GPT-5.4、Claude Opus 4.6、Gemini 2.5 Pro、Qwen 3 72B 等 8 个 LLM 骨干上的实验表明： - **高攻击成功率**：平均 ASR 达 89.4% ；即使在高风险领域（医疗、药物发现），成功率仍超过 80% 。 - **对预部署扫描器的强隐蔽性**：对 5 种单技能扫描器的通过率超过 86% ，边际检测信号 Delta le 4.7 个百分点；对 3 种跨技能联合扫描器的通过率仍超过 81% ，静态扫描器甚至完全无法检测（ Delta = 0 ）。 - **对运行时防御的高效绕过**：对 4 类运行时防御（策略门控、规则执行、风险分级、轨迹检查）的平均绕过率（DER）达 88.5% 。 ## 5. 深度机理分析与防御探索 - **不可或缺性消融**：在三技能级联上执行片段撤回实验，撤回任意单个修改后残余 ASR 骤降至 13% – 18% ，验证危害确实由技能组合承载。 - **攻击技术识别**：归纳出 9 种 recurring 技术，如**条件逻辑注入**（M1）与**认知框架重构**（M2），它们直接利用 LLM 的语义上下文特性，而非传统代码漏洞。 - **概念验证防御**：提出 **Behavior-Composition Scanner**，通过跨技能行为摘要、链式后果推理与生成式反事实判断检测级联。该防御可将因果级联的隐蔽率降低逾 30 个百分点，但对组合级联与混合级联仍有显著局限。 ## 6. 结论与启示 论文揭示了**组件级完整性**与**系统级安全性**之间的根本断层：即使每个技能单独通过安全审查，其组合仍可能产生严重危害。研究呼吁技能安全领域必须从“孤立审查单个技能”转向“推理跨技能交互”，并推动开发能够进行组合式安全分析的新一代防御机制。 Q7: 想要进一步了解论文 以上只是了解一篇论文的几个基本FAQ。如果你还想与Kimi进一步讨论该论文，请点击 这里 为你跳转Kimi AI网页版，并启动一个与该论文相关的新会话。

Authors: Zihao Zhu, Siwei Lyu, Adel Bibi, Baoyuan Wu

Categories: cs.AI

6. A Synthetic Ground-Truth Framework for the Evaluation of Explainable AI Methods

Abstract:Evaluating explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) methods is a challenging task due to the lack of reliable evaluation procedures and, in particular, the absence of ground truth explanations. In the literature, existing evaluation approaches typically assess explanations by measuring their fidelity with respect to the predictions of a black-box model. However, such evaluation strategies only quantify the degree to which an explanation reproduces the model’s output, without ensuring that the explanation correctly reflects the underlying decision process. As a consequence, different explanations may achieve similar fidelity scores while providing inconsistent or misleading interpretations of the model behavior. In this paper, we propose a framework for the evaluation of XAI methods based on synthetic ground truth. The proposed approach relies on controlled interventions to generate synthetic datasets in which the importance of input components can be determined by design. This enables the construction of ground truth explanations that are directly aligned with the behavior of the model under analysis. The framework is instantiated across three data domains, namely binary images, tabular data, and time series, allowing a comprehensive assessment of explanation methods in heterogeneous settings. Experimental results obtained by evaluating nine widely used XAI methods show significant limitations in current techniques and highlight the importance of synthetic, intervention-based benchmarks for a reliable assessment of explanation quality.

中文摘要

摘要：由于缺乏可靠的评估程序，尤其是缺少真实的解释，评估可解释人工智能（XAI）方法是一项具有挑战性的任务。在文献中，现有的评估方法通常通过测量解释与黑箱模型预测的一致性来评估解释的效果。然而，这类评估策略仅量化了解释再现模型输出的程度，而无法确保解释正确反映了潜在的决策过程。因此，不同的解释可能获得相似的保真度分数，同时提供不一致或误导性的模型行为解读。在本文中，我们提出了一个基于合成真实解释的XAI方法评估框架。所提出的方法依赖于受控干预来生成合成数据集，在这些数据集中输入组件的重要性可以通过设计确定。这使得能够构建与所分析模型行为直接一致的真实解释。该框架在三个数据领域中实施，即二值图像、表格数据和时间序列，从而允许在异构环境中对解释方法进行全面评估。通过评估九种广泛使用的XAI方法获得的实验结果显示了当前技术的显著局限性，并强调了基于合成和干预的基准在可靠评估解释质量中的重要性。

7. Predicting Transmembrane Protein Topology from 3D Structure

Abstract:This paper presents a novel approach to infer protein topology using the state-of-the-art graph neural network (GNN), SchNet. The model is trained on the same dataset used to develop the recent DeepTMHMM model with 5-fold cross-validation. Unlike the conventional approaches based on using only the protein sequences or the $\alpha$-carbons as features, we have decoded our classifier in this way, so all atom-level embeddings are used. Without applying any pre-trained weight, the final results have shown great potential that GNNs can be used for topological predictions.

中文摘要

摘要：本文提出了一种使用最先进的图神经网络（GNN）SchNet推断蛋白质拓扑结构的新方法。该模型在用于开发最近的DeepTMHMM模型的相同数据集上进行了训练，并采用了5折交叉验证。与仅使用蛋白质序列或α-碳作为特征的传统方法不同，我们以这种方式解码了我们的分类器，因此使用了所有原子级嵌入。在未应用任何预训练权重的情况下，最终结果显示GNN在拓扑预测中具有巨大潜力。

8. Spectral Feedback for Test-Time Alignment of Protein Diffusion Models

Abstract:Reward maximization alignment methods for discrete diffusion models have primarily focused on steering the reverse process, either by influencing token logits or by selecting favorable sequences at intermediate steps. These approaches largely treat inference as a unidirectional process, lacking mechanisms for revisiting undesirable token selections. We introduce Spectral Feedback, an algorithm that selects edit-positions in a feedback loop, allowing the model to iteratively correct its own generations. This approach leverages the mask structure of discrete diffusion models by re-masking and re-sampling tokens, analogous to image editing methods that reintroduce noisy latents and re-run the reverse process. While prior alignment methods focus on what token labels to assign to maximize a target reward, we instead treat which tokens to revisit as the central alignment problem. Selecting edit-positions is challenging because edit effects are interdependent: the impact of modifying one token depends on which others are edited simultaneously. We define an edit-set as a set of token positions to re-mask and re-sample. Motivated by prior work on sparse interactions in biological systems, we find empirically that edit-set value functions for protein inverse folding admit sparse Fourier representations. This structure enables Spectral Feedback to efficiently learn and optimize the value functions for edit-position selection. Spectral Feedback is model-agnostic and can be applied to pretrained, test-time aligned, and fine-tuned diffusion models. For all of these models, the algorithm improves alignment performance without modifying the underlying generative process. Applied to inverse folding with a protein stability reward oracle, it achieves a 32.3% increase in stable proteins for a pretrained model, 24.8% for Best-of-10, and 5.8% for a state-of-the-art RL fine-tuned diffusion model.

中文摘要

摘要：用于离散扩散模型的奖励最大化对齐方法主要集中于引导反向过程，要么通过影响标记 logits，要么通过在中间步骤选择有利的序列。这些方法大多将推理视为单向过程，缺乏重新审视不理想标记选择的机制。我们引入了谱反馈（Spectral Feedback）算法，该算法在反馈循环中选择编辑位置，使模型能够迭代地修正自身生成内容。这种方法利用了离散扩散模型的掩码结构，通过重新掩码和重新采样标记，类似于图像编辑方法中重新引入噪声潜量并重新运行反向过程。先前的对齐方法关注于分配哪些标记标签以最大化目标奖励，而我们则将“重新访问哪些标记”视为核心对齐问题。选择编辑位置具有挑战性，因为编辑效果是相互依赖的：修改一个标记的影响取决于同时编辑的其他标记。我们将编辑集定义为一组需要重新掩码和重新采样的标记位置。受先前关于生物系统稀疏交互研究的启发，我们通过实验证明，蛋白质逆向折叠的编辑集价值函数可以用稀疏傅里叶表示。这种结构使谱反馈能够高效地学习和优化编辑位置选择的价值函数。谱反馈与模型无关，可以应用于预训练模型、测试时对齐模型以及微调扩散模型。对于所有这些模型，该算法在不修改底层生成过程的情况下提升了对齐性能。在应用于具有蛋白质稳定性奖励预言器的逆向折叠任务时，它在预训练模型上实现了稳定蛋白数量提升32.3%，Best-of-10提升24.8%，以及在最先进的强化学习微调扩散模型上提升5.8%。

Published: 2026-09-29T02:44:44.261Z

9. Pretrained ASR Pseudo-labeling for Noisy Police Audio

Abstract:Pretrained ASR systems perform poorly on noisy Broadcast Police Communication (BPC), hindering efforts to understand police decision-making. Pseudo-labeling offers an unsupervised path to improve ASR without expensive human labels, but the efficacy of this approach on very noisy domains is not known. In this work, we systematically assess the opportunities and limits of pseudo-labeling to adapt foundation ASR models (Whisper and Qwen3-ASR) to noisy BPC domain corpora from Baltimore and Chicago. We demonstrate that existing internal confidence metrics (log-probabilities and STAR scores) fail to distinguish between high and low quality BPC pseudo-labels, and we introduce an external LLM-as-a-judge filtering paradigm that leverages parametric knowledge to discard contextually implausible transcripts. Our LLM-judging filters more aggressively than internal metrics and significantly reduces WER of the pseudo-labeled training sets across the Baltimore and Chicago BPC corpora, though a substantial gap remains relative to an oracle filter. We also introduce a new cross-model pseudo-labeling paradigm where one model is finetuned with pseudo-labels from the other, and we identify this method as a promising direction for future pseudo-labeling work.

中文摘要

摘要：预训练的自动语音识别（ASR）系统在嘈杂的广播警务通信（BPC）中表现不佳，这阻碍了对警务决策过程的理解。伪标签提供了一种无需昂贵人工标注即可改进ASR的无监督途径，但在噪声极大的领域中这种方法的有效性尚不清楚。在本研究中，我们系统地评估了伪标签在将基础ASR模型（Whisper 和 Qwen3-ASR）适应来自巴尔的摩和芝加哥的嘈杂BPC领域语料库的机会与局限性。我们证明了现有的内部置信度指标（对数概率和STAR分数）无法区分高质量和低质量的BPC伪标签，我们引入了一种外部LLM作为评判者的过滤范式，该范式利用参数化知识剔除语境上不合理的转录结果。我们的LLM评判过滤器比内部指标更为积极，有效地降低了巴尔的摩和芝加哥BPC语料库中伪标签训练集的词错误率（WER），虽然相对于理想过滤器仍存在显著差距。我们还引入了一种新的跨模型伪标签范式，即使用一个模型的伪标签来微调另一个模型，并将该方法确定为未来伪标签研究的有希望方向。

10. Do LLMs Understand Context? A Knowledge Graph-Based Evaluation Framework

Abstract:While large language models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable linguistic capabilities, a profound question lingers at their core: do these models truly comprehend context or simply excel at pattern matching on an unprecedented scale? Contextual understanding in LLMs refers to the ability to correctly extract relevant information from a given context, integrate it into a coherent internal representation, and reason over it to produce factually consistent and contextually grounded responses. However, traditional methods such as BiLingual Evaluation Understudy (BLEU) and perplexity simply measure surface-level performance. This reveals a critical gap in question answering (QA), where responses must be contextually grounded rather than simply being memorized associations. To fill this void, we propose a novel knowledge graph (KG) based evaluation framework for LLM contextual understanding in QA. Central to this is Semantic Structural Similarity for KGs (S3KG), a hybrid similarity measure combining structural and semantic signals into a single score. In addition, a diagnostic analysis framework is developed to identify and categorize reasoning errors at the triplet level, enabling fine-grained analysis of model failures. Together, across nine benchmarks, S3KG achieves F1 gains of up to $+7.6$ points over the strongest baseline and AUROC up to $0.973$.

中文摘要

摘要：虽然大型语言模型（LLMs）在语言能力上取得了显著成就，但一个深刻的问题仍然存在：这些模型是否真正理解上下文，还是仅仅在前所未有的规模上擅长模式匹配？LLMs中的上下文理解指能够从给定上下文中正确提取相关信息，将其整合到一致的内部表示中，并在此基础上进行推理，以生成在事实上一致且基于上下文的回答。然而，传统方法如双语评估替代（BLEU）和困惑度仅衡量表面性能。这揭示了问答（QA）中的一个关键差距——回答必须基于上下文，而不仅仅是记忆关联。为填补这一空白，我们提出了一种基于知识图（KG）的LLM上下文理解评估框架。在此框架的核心是知识图的语义结构相似性（S3KG），这是一种将结构信号与语义信号结合为单一分数的混合相似性度量。此外，还开发了诊断分析框架，以在三元组层面识别和分类推理错误，实现对模型失败的细粒度分析。综合来看，在九个基准测试中，S3KG相比最强基线获得了最高$+7.6$的F1增益，并实现了最高0.973的AUROC。

1. AlphaEarth distinguishes cities but compresses urban variation

Abstract:Cities differ in built form, land cover and development history, complicating comparison across places and time. Satellite foundation models map Earth’s surface onto common numerical representations. Yet the tasks and targets used to shape them typically do not focus on cities: globally consistent labels for urban function do not exist, and many datasets - especially land cover and land use classifications - collapse the built environment into few classes. Here we audit the representation, focusing on AlphaEarth but with broader applicability to other Earth embeddings, by probing the geometry and geography of embeddings for 1,000 urban areas in 162 countries. We find that cities occupy a shifted but overlapping region on the hypersphere, 62.7° from the global mean direction, and continent and climate predict 24.3% of variation among the mean directions of urban centres in excluded countries. Inside cities, degrees of urbanisation carry 8.9% of the variation, and what they leave holds shared directions whose local orientation varies, not one universal axis of urbanisation. Retained variation is itself unequal: dispersion within urban centres is 14.1% greater per standard deviation of national development, even after adjusting for population, land area and continent. Further controls suggest cities in developing countries present less contrast in vegetation and texture, and dispersion follows that contrast: full adjustment for it leaves at most 6.4% of the gradient. Annually, a city’s representation moves nearly eight times more than redrawing its own pixels explains, and contracts where the 2022 loss of Sentinel-1B removed a pass direction. AlphaEarth’s representations therefore support comparison across regions, while the differences between its annual layers are not yet validated for comparison over time.

中文摘要

摘要：城市在建筑形态、土地覆盖和发展历史上存在差异，这使得跨地点和时间的比较变得复杂。卫星基础模型将地球表面映射到通用的数值表示。然而，用于塑造这些模型的任务和目标通常并不关注城市：全球一致的城市功能标签不存在，且许多数据集——尤其是土地覆盖和土地利用分类——将建筑环境简化为少数几类。在此，我们审视了这些表示，重点关注AlphaEarth，但其适用于其他地球嵌入模型，通过探测162个国家的1000个城市区域嵌入的几何和地理特征。我们发现，城市占据了超球面上一个偏移但重叠的区域，与全球平均方向相差62.7°，且洲别和气候可以预测排除国家中城市中心平均方向变异的24.3%。在城市内部，城市化程度解释了8.9%的变异，而未解释的部分表现为共享方向，其局部取向不同，并非存在一个全球统一的城市化轴线。保留的变异本身也不均等：即使调整了人口、土地面积和洲别后，城市中心内部的离散度仍比国家发展水平的标准差高14.1%。进一步的控制显示，发展中国家的城市在植被和纹理上对比度较低，离散度随该对比度变化：完全调整后，最大仅留下6.4%的梯度。每年，城市的表示移动几乎是仅重新绘制自身像素所解释变化的八倍，并且在2022年Sentinel-1B丢失通道方向时出现收缩。因此，AlphaEarth的表示支持跨地区比较，而其年度层之间的差异尚未被验证用于时间序列比较。

2. LiTe-GS: Oracle-Efficient Next Best View Selection for 3D Gaussian Splatting

Abstract:Selecting informative camera views is critical for efficient training and adaptive refinement in 3D Gaussian Splatting, where each observation significantly influences model parameters. However, information-driven view-selection strategies can require repeated evaluations of expensive information-gain oracles as the number of candidate views increases. We propose LiTe-GS, an oracle-efficient method for next best view selection in 3D Gaussian Splatting. LiTe-GS reduces the number of information-oracle evaluations by performing randomized subset evaluation of candidate views rather than exhaustively scoring the full candidate pool. The resulting approach achieves expected $O(M\log(1/\epsilon))$ oracle complexity with respect to the number of candidate views $M$, independent of the selection cardinality $K$, while providing an explicit trade-off between oracle efficiency and approximation quality through $\epsilon$. We provide theoretical guarantees on oracle complexity and approximation performance under the proposed selection scheme. Experiments on Blender and Mip-NeRF 360 demonstrate that LiTe-GS maintains reconstruction quality comparable to Fisher-information-based baselines while substantially reducing the number of Fisher-oracle evaluations across different acquisition settings.

中文摘要

摘要：选择信息型摄像头视图对于三维高斯散画中的高效训练和自适应优化至关重要，因为每次观测都显著影响模型参数。然而，信息驱动的视图选择策略可能需要反复评估昂贵的信息增益预言机，随着候选视角数量的增加。我们提出了LiTe-GS，这是一种在3D高斯散画中选择次优视图的oracle高效方法。LiTe-GS通过对候选视图进行随机子集评估，而非对整个候选池进行详尽评分，从而减少信息预言机评估次数。最终的方法实现了预言机复杂度相对于候选视角数量$O$M$的预期，且不依赖于选择基数$K$，同时通过$\epsilon$在预言机效率与近似质量之间实现了明确权衡。我们为所提选择方案下的oracle复杂度和近似性能提供理论保证。Blender和Mip-NeRF 360的实验表明，LiTe-GS在重建质量上与基于Fisher信息的基线相当，同时大幅减少不同采集环境中的Fisher-oracle评估次数。

3. CSCWD: Cross-Scale Channel-wise Knowledge Distillation for Lightweight Tiny Object Detection on Edge Devices

Abstract:Real-time tiny object detection in aerial imagery is constrained by the weak spatial evidence of very small objects and the loss of high-resolution detail in lightweight detectors. This study presents Cross-Scale Channel-wise Knowledge Distillation (CSCWD), a training-time framework that transfers high-resolution spatial representations from a YOLO11m-P2 teacher to a compact YOLO11n student without altering the student’s inference architecture. Unlike conventional same-scale feature distillation, CSCWD transfers supervision from teacher P2 to student P3 after feature alignment while retaining same-scale distillation at deeper pyramid levels. Under the unified seven-sequence Drone-vs-Bird validation protocol, YOLO11n-CSCWD achieves 50.17% mean average precision at an intersection-over-union threshold of 0.5 (mAP@0.5) and 59.73% recall, improving the matched CA-YOLO11n baseline by 2.92 percentage points in mAP@0.5 and 3.55 points in recall. Cross-scale alignment further increases mAP@0.5 by 2.09 points over the corresponding same-scale channel-wise distillation configuration. In zero-shot evaluation on DUT-Anti-UAV, mAP@0.5 increases from 48.29% to 50.06% without target-domain fine-tuning. This domain was included because its challenging small targets make low-latency, computationally efficient detection particularly relevant. On Raspberry Pi 5 using NCNN-FP16 at 640x640 resolution, the 2.58-million-parameter student achieves 50.32% mAP@0.5 at 82.32 ms mean wall-clock latency, or 12.15 frames per second, while retaining essentially the same runtime and memory requirements as the matched baseline. The results support cross-scale distillation for improving tiny-target detection without increasing inference-time model complexity.

中文摘要

摘要：航空影像中实时微小物体检测受限于微小物体的弱空间特征以及轻量级检测器中高分辨率细节的丢失。本研究提出了跨尺度通道知识蒸馏（CSCWD），这是一个训练时框架，可将 YOLO11m-P2 教师模型的高分辨率空间表示传递给紧凑型 YOLO11n 学生模型，而不改变学生模型的推理架构。不同于传统的同尺度特征蒸馏，CSCWD 在特征对齐后将教师 P2 的监督传递给学生 P3，同时在更深的金字塔层保留同尺度蒸馏。在统一的七序列 Drone-vs-Bird 验证协议下，YOLO11n-CSCWD 在交并比阈值为 0.5 的条件下实现了 50.17% 的平均精度（mAP@0.5）和 59.73% 的召回率，相比匹配的 CA-YOLO11n 基线，mAP@0.5 提高了 2.92 个百分点，召回率提高了 3.55 个百分点。跨尺度对齐相比对应的同尺度通道蒸馏配置进一步将 mAP@0.5 提高了 2.09 个百分点。在 DUT-Anti-UAV 的零样本评估中，mAP@0.5 从 48.29% 提升至 50.06%，无需目标域微调。选择该数据集是因为其具有挑战性的微小目标使得低延迟且计算高效的检测尤其重要。在使用 NCNN-FP16、分辨率为 640x640 的 Raspberry Pi 5 上，这一拥有 2.58 百万个参数的学生模型实现了 50.32% 的 mAP@0.5，并且平均墙钟延迟为 82.32 毫秒，即每秒 12.15 帧，同时基本保持与匹配基线相同的运行时和内存需求。结果支持跨尺度蒸馏在不增加推理时模型复杂度的前提下提高微小目标检测效果。

4. What Improves Multimodal Misinformation Detection? Answers from a Large-Scale Empirical Study

Abstract:Multimodal misinformation is increasingly crafted to look convincing by pairing a textual claim with an image that appears to “prove” it. Yet in practice, building effective detectors often hinges on a small set of design choices that are rarely examined in a controlled way. In this paper, we conduct a large-scale study of multimodal design choices for misinformation detection with over 3,375 experiments- spanning three benchmark datasets and a broad range of pre-trained vision and language backbones. Through systematic comparisons and targeted robustness analyses, we distill practical guidance on which design choices help, when do they fail silently, and what aspects of the pipeline most strongly shape model behavior, answering 4 key Research Questions (RQs). We aim to provide a reliable foundation for designing stronger and more dependable multimodal misinformation detection systems, thus contributing to the broader research community.

中文摘要

摘要：多模态虚假信息越来越多地通过将文本声明与看似“证明”该声明的图像配对来让人信服。然而在实际操作中，构建有效的检测器通常依赖于一小部分设计选择，而这些选择很少以受控方式进行研究。在本文中，我们进行了大规模的多模态虚假信息检测设计选择研究，共进行了超过3,375次实验——涵盖三个基准数据集以及广泛的预训练视觉和语言骨干网络。通过系统比较和有针对性的稳健性分析，我们提炼出了实际指导，说明哪些设计选择有效、它们何时会悄然失效，以及管道的哪些方面最强烈地影响模型行为，回答了四个关键研究问题（RQs）。我们的目标是为设计更强大、更可靠的多模态虚假信息检测系统提供可靠基础，从而为更广泛的研究社区做出贡献。

5. ProCAP: Probabilistic Cross-Attentive Prompt Learning for Vision-Language Models

Abstract:Pre-trained vision-language models such as CLIP can recognize new categories via prompting, but they often struggle when labeled data are scarce or the test distribution shifts. Prompt learning adapts only a small set of parameters while keeping the backbone frozen, yet many existing multimodal prompt learners couple the visual and textual branches weakly and can be brittle in low-shot regimes. We propose ProCAP, a probabilistic cross-attentive prompt learning framework that improves cross-modal interaction and training stability without updating any CLIP weights: it learns both visual and textual prompt tokens and links them through stacked bidirectional multi-head cross-attention so the two branches refine each other across prompt depth. To reduce overfitting under limited supervision, we parameterize prompt tokens with Gaussian means and variances and regularize them with lightweight KL and L2 penalties, and we further add a compact symmetric InfoNCE head that aligns cross-attended image features with class-level text representations in a shared low-dimensional space. Across few-shot base-to-novel generalization on 11 datasets, cross-dataset transfer, and domain generalization on ImageNet shift benchmarks, ProCAP achieves strong aggregate base-to-novel performance and competitive transfer performance while keeping the CLIP backbone unchanged.

中文摘要

摘要：预训练的视觉-语言模型如CLIP可以通过提示（prompting）识别新类别，但在标注数据稀缺或测试分布发生变化时，它们往往表现不佳。提示学习仅调整少量参数，同时保持主干网络固定，但现有许多多模态提示学习方法在视觉分支和文本分支的耦合较弱，在低样本情境下可能表现不稳。我们提出ProCAP，一种概率性跨注意力提示学习框架，无需更新任何CLIP权重即可提升跨模态交互和训练稳定性：它同时学习视觉和文本提示令牌，并通过堆叠的双向多头跨注意力将二者关联，使两个分支在提示深度上相互优化。为了在有限监督下减少过拟合，我们使用高斯均值和方差对提示令牌参数化，并通过轻量KL和L2惩罚进行正则化；此外，我们进一步添加一个紧凑对称的InfoNCE头，将跨注意力处理后的图像特征与类级文本表示在共享低维空间中对齐。在11个数据集上的少样本基础到新类别泛化、跨数据集迁移以及ImageNet shift基准的域泛化实验中，ProCAP在保持CLIP主干不变的情况下，实现了强劲的整体基础到新类别表现和有竞争力的迁移性能。

6. LensDesigner: A Self-Improving Agent for Optical Lens Design

Abstract:Optical lens design is a complex, non-convex optimization challenge that relies heavily on human experience and intuition. Existing optimized-based automatic lens design methods struggle to navigate this vast parameter space without meticulous manual tuning. In this paper, we present LensDesigner, an autonomous agent framework that mirrors the problem-solving workflow of expert opticians. To overcome the initial cold start problem, we construct LensLib100K, an extensive optical lens library, and employ Optics-Aware Retrieval to supply physically valid structural seeds. Within an interactive physical simulation environment, the agent executes macroscopic orchestration while receiving immediate optical feedback. Furthermore, we introduce a continuous self-evolving mechanism guided by a curriculum agent. By iteratively solving design tasks with progressively increasing difficulty, the agent autonomously extracts, accumulates, and reuses design heuristics, effectively evolving its optical lens design expertise over time. At the evaluation level, we introduce LensArena, a standardized evaluation benchmark comprising $120$ diverse optical design tasks, covering extreme configurations. Extensive experiments on this benchmark demonstrate that LensDesigner significantly outperforms publicly available baseline algorithms, achieving superior success rates and optimization efficiency. We hope this work sheds light on the emerging field of intelligent optics. The code will be publicly available.

中文摘要

摘要：光学镜头设计是一项复杂的非凸优化挑战，极度依赖人类的经验和直觉。现有基于优化的自动镜头设计方法在没有精细手动调节的情况下，很难在这个庞大的参数空间中进行探索。在本文中，我们提出了 LensDesigner，一种自主代理框架，模拟专家光学师的问题解决流程。为了克服初期冷启动问题，我们构建了 LensLib100K，一个大型光学镜头库，并采用光学感知检索（Optics-Aware Retrieval）提供物理上有效的结构初始值。在交互式物理仿真环境中，代理执行宏观协调，同时获得即时的光学反馈。此外，我们引入了由课程代理指导的连续自我演化机制。通过迭代解决难度逐步增加的设计任务，代理能够自主提取、积累并重用设计启发式方法，有效地随时间演化其光学镜头设计专长。在评估层面，我们引入了 LensArena，一个标准化评估基准，包含 120 个多样化的光学设计任务，覆盖极端配置。在该基准上进行的大量实验表明，LensDesigner 显著优于公开可用的基线算法，实现了更高的成功率和优化效率。我们希望这项工作为新兴的智能光学领域提供启示。代码将公开发布。

7. The Shape of Events: Edge-Based Inductive Biases via Cross-Domain Distillation

Abstract:Convolutional neural networks trained on ImageNet are known to exhibit a strong preference for local high-frequency texture, an inductive bias that translates into fragile robustness against distribution shifts in real-world environments. Event cameras, in contrast, record only changes in scene brightness and are therefore well suited to capturing contour information; however, due to the absence of diagnostic benchmarks in the event domain, the inductive bias that event-camera data instills in vision models has remained underexplored. In this work, we use knowledge distillation from the event domain to the RGB domain so as to exploit the rich evaluation toolkit available in the RGB domain and systematically dissect this inductive bias. Our experiments show that distillation from the event domain induces, in the RGB domain, color invariance, shape bias, and robustness to high-frequency noise. We identify the underlying mechanism as the model suppressing its dependence on high-frequency texture while acquiring a stronger dependence on edge-based object shape. This hypothesis is supported by changes in how color and spatial information are processed at the early layers, together with a spectral trade-off in which robustness to the absence of high-frequency components coexists with vulnerability to contamination of the relied-upon frequency bands and to disruption of geometric structure. We further show that this inductive bias differs from existing robustification methods and that it functions as a useful prior for diverse downstream tasks in which shape and contour information contribute alongside other cues. The code is available at this https URL .

中文摘要

摘要：在 ImageNet 上训练的卷积神经网络已知会表现出对局部高频纹理的强烈偏好，这种归纳偏置导致其在现实环境中的分布变化下表现出脆弱的鲁棒性。相比之下，事件相机仅记录场景亮度的变化，因此非常适合捕捉轮廓信息；然而，由于事件领域缺乏诊断基准，事件相机数据在视觉模型中所引入的归纳偏置仍未得到充分研究。在本工作中，我们通过将事件领域的知识蒸馏到 RGB 领域，以利用 RGB 领域丰富的评估工具，并系统地解析这种归纳偏置。我们的实验表明，从事件领域进行蒸馏会在 RGB 领域诱导出颜色不变性、形状偏置以及对高频噪声的鲁棒性。我们确定其潜在机制为模型在获取基于边缘的对象形状更强依赖的同时，抑制对高频纹理的依赖。这一假设得到了早期层中颜色和空间信息处理方式变化的支持，同时伴随着频谱权衡，即对高频成分缺失的鲁棒性与对所依赖频段污染和几何结构破坏的脆弱性共存。我们进一步表明，这种归纳偏置不同于现有的稳健化方法，它可作为对多样下游任务有用的先验，在这些任务中，形状和轮廓信息与其他线索共同发挥作用。代码可在此 https URL 获取。

8. Atlases Are Already Inside: Recovering Population Templates from Pretrained Diffusion Models

Abstract:We present a new inference-time sampler for diffusion models that gives a pretrained model a capability it was never trained for: constructing the atlas of the population it synthesizes. The sampler converges from every random seed to the population’s central anatomy, which we call the \emph{intrinsic atlas}. The advantage is threefold. (1) It requires no retraining. A diffusion model that has already learned a coherent population, including the released ones, yields its atlas in a single inference pass without involving deformable registration. (2) It applies to multiple domains, such as brain MRI, chest X-ray, faces, and 3D shapes. (3) It extends to subpopulations. One age-conditioned model gives an atlas at any age in its training range, and the resulting family reproduces the CSF expansion of healthy aging. Evaluated as a registration target, the intrinsic atlas is best or second-best on every dataset against classical and learned templates, and the most central template on held-out brain MRI cohorts. Atlas construction can be reframed as a byproduct of generative modeling: a diffusion model is a learned representation of population structure, and the atlas is what it already contains.

中文摘要

摘要：我们提出了一种用于扩散模型的新推理时间采样器，它赋予了预训练模型一种其从未训练过的能力：构建其生成的人群的图谱。该采样器能从任意随机种子收敛到人群的中心解剖结构，我们称之为\emph{内在图谱}。其优势有三方面：(1) 无需重新训练。一个已经学会了连贯人群的扩散模型，包括已经发布的模型，只需一次推理即可生成其图谱，无需涉及可变形配准。(2) 适用于多个领域，例如脑部MRI、胸部X光、面部以及三维形状。(3) 可扩展到子人群。一个按年龄条件训练的模型可以生成其训练范围内任何年龄的图谱，生成的系列能够再现健康衰老中的脑脊液扩张。作为配准目标评估时，内在图谱在每个数据集上都相较于经典和学习型模板表现最佳或第二佳，并且在未见脑MRI队列中是最中心的模板。图谱构建可以重新定义为生成建模的副产物：扩散模型是一种对人群结构的学习表示，而图谱正是它已经包含的内容。

9. Action Forcing: Training World Models on Unsupervised Video by Recovering Underlying Egomotion Bases

Abstract:Synchronised action annotations are needed to train controllable world models and these datasets remain elusive. Existing approaches make use of instrumented platforms with calibrated sensors, costly manual annotation, or latent-action models which lack grounding. We instead turn ordinary unlabelled video into action-supervised training data by recovering (without training) a data-derived egomotion basis. We track pixel displacements across frames and exploit the recurring coherent structure induced by egomotion to obtain grounded control signals directly. Using a method as simple as principal components analysis perform this, we find that the leading components provide signed, scalable, and composable throttle—yaw controls, although the method can recover only motion axes represented in the data. To prevent a high-capacity video DiT from exploiting pixel-level supervision, an online latent critic distils a frozen decoder—tracker—PCA (Principal Components Analysis) teacher without backpropagating through the decoder or tracker. Finally we critique the use of video generation metrics to evaluate WMs and introduce an example of an alternative, reference-free evaluation method. We measure \textit{controllability}, \textit{plausibility}, \textit{conjuring} (creating objects out of thin air) and \textit{geometric integrity}, revealing failures that conventional video metrics miss. We show that most baselines follow familiar action directions but struggle to reverse or remain stationary. Our model handles both while retaining compositional control and generation quality. Despite backwards actions being less than $1\%$ of our training data, we find that the model learns to reverse, scale its response linearly, and compose throttle with steering, all simply by learning through a grounded action space.

中文摘要

摘要：训练可控世界模型需要同步动作标注，但这类数据集仍然难以获得。现有方法依赖配备校准传感器的仪器化平台、昂贵的手工标注，或者缺乏基础的潜在动作模型。我们则将普通未标注的视频转化为动作监督训练数据，方法是通过恢复（无需训练）数据衍生的自运动（egomotion）基。我们追踪帧间的像素位移，并利用自运动引起的重复一致结构，直接获得有基础的控制信号。使用像主成分分析（PCA）这样简单的方法进行这一操作，我们发现主要成分提供有符号、可扩展并可组合的油门——偏航控制，但该方法只能恢复数据中存在的运动轴。为了防止高容量视频DiT利用像素级监督，在线潜在评估器（latent critic）蒸馏了一个冻结的解码器——追踪器——PCA教师模型，而无需通过解码器或追踪器进行反向传播。最后，我们批评了使用视频生成指标来评估世界模型的做法，并提出了一个替代的、无参考的评估方法。我们测量了可控性、合理性、创作力（凭空生成物体）和几何完整性，揭示了传统视频指标无法检测的失败情况。实验表明，大多数基线模型在熟悉的动作方向上表现正常，但难以执行倒退或保持静止。我们的模型在保证组合控制和生成质量的同时，能够处理两者。尽管倒退动作在训练数据中不到1%，我们发现模型仍通过学习有基础的动作空间能够学会倒退、线性缩放响应，并将油门与转向组合使用。

10. MVAgent: Multi-Agent Video Generation via Consistent Condition Construction and Shot-Level Policy Optimization

Abstract:Multi-shot agentic video generation requires consistent character appearance, stable spatial layout across camera angles, and continuous character state between shots. When every shot is a separate request to a frozen generator, repeated text does not determine appearance, layout or state. We therefore recast the problem as condition construction and present MVAgent, a multi-agent pipeline whose agents collaborate through typed conditioning inputs. Because an environment image shows one viewpoint, a Spatial Grounding agent samples views from generated camera-traversal clips and anchors each shot to the view matching its framing. As generated shots drift from the plan, an Observer records how each shot ends in a continuity memory, from which a Transition agent builds character action and spatial references for the next shot. An Orchestrator composes these inputs into each request. Since a request reveals its effect only after rendering, we train it by agentic reinforcement learning with Trunk-GDPO, which compares rendered candidates at every shot rather than once per video and continues the best as the trunk. With generator and judges frozen, MVAgent attains the highest cross-shot consistency and narrative-planning quality among the compared methods on ViMax-Bench and is preferred over the strongest agentic baseline in human evaluation.

中文摘要

摘要：多镜头代理视频生成需要角色外观一致、不同摄像机角度下空间布局稳定，以及镜头之间角色状态连续。当每个镜头都是对固定生成器的单独请求时，重复的文本无法决定外观、布局或状态。因此，我们将该问题重新定义为条件构建，并提出了 MVAgent，一种多代理流水线，其代理通过类型化条件输入进行协作。由于环境图像仅显示一个视角，空间定位代理从生成的摄像机遍历片段中采样视角，并将每个镜头固定到与其构图匹配的视角。当生成镜头偏离计划时，观察者会在连续性记忆中记录每个镜头的结尾，从中过渡代理为下一镜头构建角色动作和空间参考。编排者将这些输入汇总到每个请求中。由于请求的效果只有在渲染后才能显现，我们通过使用 Trunk-GDPO 的代理强化学习对其进行训练，该方法在每个镜头比较渲染候选而非每个视频进行一次比较，并将表现最佳者作为主干继续。固定生成器和评审器后，MVAgent 在 ViMax-Bench 上在跨镜头一致性和叙事规划质量方面达到最高，并在人类评估中优于最强的代理基线方法。
